Original source text:
(vi) Design ice forces calculated according to I2.3.2.1 (iv) are applicable for ships which are assigned the Polar Class PC6 or PC7 and have a bow form with vertical sides. This includes bows where the normal frame angles beta' at the considered sub-regions, as defined in I2.3.2.1 (i), are between 0 and 10 deg.
Normalized requirement:
(vi) Design ice forces calculated according to I2.3.2.1 (iv) are applicable for ships which are assigned the Polar Class PC6 or PC7 and have a bow form with vertical sides. This includes bows where the normal frame angles beta' at the considered sub-regions, as defined in I2.3.2.1 (i), are between 0 and 10 deg.

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-010> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc1
            nltl:polarClassPc2
            nltl:polarClassPc3
            nltl:polarClassPc4
            nltl:polarClassPc5
            nltl:polarClassPc6
            nltl:polarClassPc7
        ) ;
        sh:message "A ship must identify one permitted Polar Class value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalSidedBowForm ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must record whether it has a vertical-sided bow form."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowVerticalSideStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForceMethod ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:polarClassPc6
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:verticalSidedBowForm ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:normalFrameAngle ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-010-normal-frame-angle>
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:polarClassPc7
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:verticalSidedBowForm ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:normalFrameAngle ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-010-normal-frame-angle>
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:polarClassPc1
                    nltl:polarClassPc2
                    nltl:polarClassPc3
                    nltl:polarClassPc4
                    nltl:polarClassPc5
                )
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:verticalSidedBowForm ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "For a PC6 or PC7 ship with a vertical-sided bow, the normal frame angle must be between 0 and 10 degrees."
.

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-010-normal-frame-angle> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 0 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 10 ;
        sh:message "The normal frame angle must be between 0 and 10 degrees."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;

    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:I2_010_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc6
            nltl:polarClassPc7
        ) ;
        sh:message "The ship must have Polar Class PC6 or PC7 for this design ice-force applicability rule."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForceMethod ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The applicable design ice-force calculation method must be recorded."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:verticalSidedBowForm ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:bowVerticalSideStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:normalFrameAngle ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node gs:I2_010_NormalFrameAngleShape
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "The ship must represent a vertical-sided bow, or provide normal frame angles between 0 and 10 degrees for the considered bow sub-regions." .

gs:I2_010_NormalFrameAngleShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 0 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 10 ;
        sh:message "The normal frame angle must be between 0 and 10 degrees, inclusive."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/DEG> ;
        sh:message "The normal frame angle must use degrees."
    ] .

```

Original source text:
For category A and B ships other than oil tankers constructed on or after 1 January 2017, all cargo tanks constructed and utilized to carry oil shall be separated from the outer shell by a distance of not less than 0.76 m.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND ship_type != oil_tanker AND construction_date >= '2017-01-01' AND cargo_tank_carries_oil = true, THEN cargo_tank_outer_shell_separation_m >= 0.76.
Required inputs: Ship category/type/date and cargo-tank use/separation measurements

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:IMO_109_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:cargoTankCarriesOil ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:cargoTankOuterShellSeparation ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an applicable Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017, other than an oil tanker, carrying oil in cargo tanks, cargo tank outer shell separation must be at least 0.76 m." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:shipType ?shipType ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:cargoTankCarriesOil true .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                )
                FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date)
                FILTER (LCASE(STR(?shipType)) != "oil tanker")
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this a nltl:oilTanker .
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:cargoTankOuterShellSeparation ?separation .
                    ?separation a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                                qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                                qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value >= "0.76"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-109-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarShipCategoryA
            nltl:polarShipCategoryB
            nltl:polarShipCategoryC
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship category of A, B, or C must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship type must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A valid construction date must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:cargoTankCarriesOil ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Whether the cargo tank carries oil must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For a Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017, other than an oil tanker, each applicable cargo-tank outer-shell separation must be at least 0.76 m." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:shipType ?shipType ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:cargoTankCarriesOil ?carriesOil .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                )
                FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date)
                FILTER (?carriesOil = true)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this a nltl:oilTanker .
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:cargoTankOuterShellSeparation ?separation .
                    ?separation qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                                qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
                    FILTER (?value >= "0.76"^^xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-109> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipCategory ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCategoryValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship category is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship type is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A construction date is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:cargoTankCarriesOil ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The cargo-tank oil-service status is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an applicable Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017, which is not an oil tanker and whose cargo tanks carry oil, cargo-tank outer-shell separation must be at least 0.76 m." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
          nltl:shipType ?shipType ;
          nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
          nltl:cargoTankCarriesOil ?carriesOil .

    FILTER (?category IN (
        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
    ))
    FILTER (?shipType != "oil_tanker")
    FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date)
    FILTER (?carriesOil = true)
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this rdf:type nltl:oilTanker
    }

    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:cargoTankOuterShellSeparation ?separation .
        ?separation qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                    qudt:unit unit:M .
        FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
        FILTER (?value >= "0.76"^^xsd:decimal)
    }
}
""" ;
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO125ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO125NonPolarBranch
        gen:IMO125PolarBranch
    ) ;
    sh:message "The ship must identify whether it operates in polar waters and, when applicable, record polar-operation coverage in the Garbage Record Book, Garbage Management Plan, and garbage placards." .

gen:IMO125NonPolarBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInPolarWaters ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:message "shipOperatesInPolarWaters must not be recorded as true for the non-applicable branch."
    ] ;
    sh:not [
        a sh:NodeShape ;
        sh:property [
            sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInPolarWaters ;
            sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
            sh:hasValue true
        ]
    ] .

gen:IMO125PolarBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInPolarWaters ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "shipOperatesInPolarWaters must be recorded as true for the applicable branch."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:garbageRecordBookPolarCoverage ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Polar-operation coverage must be recorded in the applicable Garbage Record Book."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:garbageManagementPlanPolarCoverage ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Polar-operation coverage must be recorded in the applicable Garbage Management Plan."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:garbagePlacardPolarCoverage ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Polar-operation coverage must be recorded in the applicable garbage placards."
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO26-001> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:solasChapterICertified ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "SOLAS chapter I certification status is required to establish Polar Code chapter 9 applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCodeChapter9Applicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Polar Code chapter 9 may apply only to a ship certified in accordance with SOLAS chapter I." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:polarCodeChapter9Applicable ?applicable .
                FILTER ( ?applicable = true )
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:solasChapterICertified ?certified .
                    FILTER ( ?certified = true )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Where the regulations of this chapter are achieved by means of adding devices requiring a source of power, this source shall be able to operate independently of the ship's main source of power.
Normalized requirement:

Required inputs: Device inventory, power requirement, and power-source topology

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                $this nltl:addedLifeSavingDeviceRequiresPower ?requiresPower .
                FILTER (?requiresPower = true)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:devicePowerSourceIndependentOfMainPower ?independent .
                    FILTER (?independent = true)
                }
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_070_ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipType ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue "passenger_ship" ;
                sh:message "A passenger ship must identify ship type as passenger_ship."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:sizeCompatibilityStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Each assigned survival-equipment item must be properly sized."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasPersonOnBoardMember ;
                sh:class nltl:personOnBoardMember ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:hasPersonalSurvivalEquipmentAssignment ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:class nltl:personalSurvivalEquipmentAssignment ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:assignedPersonalSurvivalEquipment ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:class nltl:personalSurvivalEquipment
                            ]
                        ]
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Each person on board a passenger ship must have a properly sized immersion suit or thermal protective aid assignment." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:shipType ?shipType .
                        FILTER (?shipType = "passenger_ship")
                        $this nltl:hasPersonOnBoardMember ?person .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?person nltl:hasPersonalSurvivalEquipmentAssignment ?assignment .
                            ?assignment nltl:assignedPersonalSurvivalEquipment ?equipment .
                            ?equipment rdf:type ?equipmentType .
                            FILTER (?equipmentType IN (
                                nltl:immersionSuitEquipment,
                                nltl:thermalProtectiveAidEquipment
                            ))
                            $this nltl:sizeCompatibilityStatus true .
                        }
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipType ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:pattern "^(?!passenger_ship$).+" ;
                sh:message "The ship type must be present to establish that the passenger-ship condition does not apply."
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-070> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Each person on board a passenger ship must have an assigned immersion suit or thermal protective aid." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this a nltl:passengerShip ;
                      nltl:hasPersonOnBoardMember ?person .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?person nltl:hasPersonalSurvivalEquipmentAssignment ?assignment .
                    ?assignment nltl:assignedPersonalSurvivalEquipment ?equipment .
                    ?equipment rdf:type ?equipmentType .
                    FILTER (?equipmentType IN (
                        nltl:immersionSuitEquipment,
                        nltl:thermalProtectiveAidEquipment
                    ))
                }
            }
        """ ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A passenger ship must record that the assigned survival equipment is properly sized." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            SELECT $this WHERE {
                $this a nltl:passengerShip .
                {
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:sizeCompatibilityStatus ?status .
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:sizeCompatibilityStatus ?status .
                    FILTER (?status != true)
                }
            }
        """ ;
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF063ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:node gen:TRF063TwinShipShape
        ]
        [
            sh:node gen:TRF063TripleShipShape
        ]
        [
            sh:node gen:TRF063NonApplicableLowCountShape
        ]
        [
            sh:node gen:TRF063NonApplicableHighCountShape
        ]
    ) .

gen:TRF063TwinShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:minInclusive 2 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 2
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingIceStrengtheningExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shellIceStrengtheningExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSidePropellerStrengtheningEnvelope ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:sidePropellerStrengtheningEnvelope ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063EnvelopeShape
    ] .

gen:TRF063TripleShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:minInclusive 3 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 3
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:framingIceStrengtheningExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shellIceStrengtheningExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSidePropellerStrengtheningEnvelope ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:sidePropellerStrengtheningEnvelope ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063EnvelopeShape
    ] .

gen:TRF063NonApplicableLowCountShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:maxInclusive 1
    ] .

gen:TRF063NonApplicableHighCountShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:minInclusive 4
    ] .

gen:TRF063EnvelopeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengtheningEnvelopeBoundary ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:tankTop
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengtheningForwardExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063ForwardExtentQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengtheningAftExtent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063AftExtentQuantityShape
    ] .

gen:TRF063ForwardExtentQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minInclusive 1.5
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M>
    ] .

gen:TRF063AftExtentQuantityShape
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF063ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:TRF063ApplicableShipBranch
        gen:TRF063NonApplicableShipBranch
    ) .

gen:TRF063ApplicableShipBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 2 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 3 ;
        sh:message "A twin- or triple-screw ship must have propellerCount 2 or 3."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSidePropellerStrengtheningEnvelope ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:sidePropellerStrengtheningEnvelope ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063StrengtheningEnvelopeShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A twin- or triple-screw ship must provide a side-propeller strengthening envelope."
    ] .

gen:TRF063NonApplicableShipBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [
                sh:maxInclusive 1
            ]
            [
                sh:minInclusive 4
            ]
        ) ;
        sh:message "A ship is outside this requirement when propellerCount is not 2 or 3."
    ] .

gen:TRF063StrengtheningEnvelopeShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengtheningEnvelopeBoundary ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:tankTop ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The strengthening envelope boundary must be a tank top."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengtheningForwardExtent ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The strengthening envelope must extend at least 1.5 metres forward."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:strengtheningAftExtent ;
        sh:node gen:TRF063LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The strengthening envelope must extend at least 1.5 metres aft."
    ] .

gen:TRF063LengthQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive "1.5"^^xsd:decimal ;
        sh:message "The extent must be at least 1.5 metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The extent must use metres as its unit."
    ] .

```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix nltlg: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

nltlg:ForceMegaNewtonShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaN> ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

nltlg:ForceKiloNewtonShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

nltlg:DimensionlessRatioShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

nltlg:LoadPatchAspectRatio13Shape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:minInclusive 1.3 ;
        sh:maxInclusive 1.3
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

nltlg:ApplicableBowDesignForceShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bulbousBowPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc6
            nltl:polarClassPc7
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIii ;
        sh:node nltlg:ForceMegaNewtonShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIv ;
        sh:node nltlg:ForceMegaNewtonShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shapeCoefficient ;
        sh:node nltlg:DimensionlessRatioShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shapeCoefficient ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive 0.6 ;
                sh:maxInclusive 0.6
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchAspectRatio ;
        sh:node nltlg:LoadPatchAspectRatio13Shape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForce ;
        sh:node nltlg:ForceKiloNewtonShape ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For an applicable PC6 or PC7 bulbous-bow ship, bowDesignForce must be at least both referenced bow design forces; MegaNewton values are compared after conversion to KiloNewton, with the part-(iii) reference force based on fa = 0.6 and AR = 1.3." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bulbousBowPresent true ;
                      nltl:polarClass ?polarClass ;
                      nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIii ?forceIii ;
                      nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIv ?forceIv ;
                      nltl:shapeCoefficient ?shapeCoefficient ;
                      nltl:loadPatchAspectRatio ?loadPatchAspectRatio ;
                      nltl:bowDesignForce ?designForce .

                FILTER (
                    ?polarClass IN (
                        nltl:polarClassPc6,
                        nltl:polarClassPc7
                    )
                )

                ?forceIii qudt:numericValue ?numericIii ;
                          qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaN> .
                ?forceIv qudt:numericValue ?numericIv ;
                         qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/MegaN> .
                ?shapeCoefficient qudt:numericValue ?numericShape ;
                                   qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                ?loadPatchAspectRatio qudt:numericValue ?numericAspectRatio ;
                                       qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                ?designForce qudt:numericValue ?numericDesign ;
                             qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> .

                FILTER (
                    ?numericShape = 0.6 &&
                    ?numericAspectRatio = 1.3
                )

                FILTER (
                    ?numericDesign < (?numericIii * 1000) ||
                    ?numericDesign < (?numericIv * 1000)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

nltlg:NonApplicableBulbousBowShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bulbousBowPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] .

nltlg:NonApplicablePolarClassShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc1
            nltl:polarClassPc2
            nltl:polarClassPc3
            nltl:polarClassPc4
            nltl:polarClassPc5
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] .

nltlg:I2_011_BowDesignForceShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bulbousBowPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:minCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        nltlg:ApplicableBowDesignForceShape
        nltlg:NonApplicableBulbousBowShape
        nltlg:NonApplicablePolarClassShape
    ) .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:I2_011_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        gen:I2_011_ApplicableShape
        gen:I2_011_BulbousBowAbsentShape
        gen:I2_011_NonPolarClassShape
    ) .

gen:I2_011_ApplicableShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:class nltl:polarClassValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:polarClassPc6
            nltl:polarClassPc7
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship subject to the PC6 or PC7 bulbous-bow rule must have exactly one applicable polar class."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bulbousBowPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship subject to the applicable rule must record that a bulbous bow is present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIii ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:MegaN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The I2.3.2.1(iii) bow design force must be supplied as a single force quantity in MN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIv ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:MegaN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The I2.3.2.1(iv) bow design force must be supplied as a single force quantity in MN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shapeCoefficient ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The shape coefficient must be supplied as a single dimensionless quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:loadPatchAspectRatio ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minInclusive 1.3 ;
                sh:maxInclusive 1.3 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "For the lower-bound calculation, the load-patch aspect ratio must be exactly 1.3 and dimensionless."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bowDesignForce ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue qudt:KiloN ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The bow design force must be supplied as a single force quantity in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The bow design force must equal, within numerical tolerance, the greater of the I2.3.2.1(iii) and I2.3.2.1(iv) force values, converted from MN to kN." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIii ?iiiNode ;
                      nltl:bowDesignForceMethodI2Point3Point2Point1PartIv ?ivNode ;
                      nltl:bowDesignForce ?resultNode .

                ?iiiNode qudt:numericValue ?iii ;
                         qudt:unit qudt:MegaN .
                ?ivNode qudt:numericValue ?iv ;
                        qudt:unit qudt:MegaN .
                ?resultNode qudt:numericValue ?result ;
                            qudt:unit qudt:KiloN .

                BIND(IF(?iii >= ?iv, ?iii, ?iv) * 1000.0 AS ?expected)

                FILTER (
                    (?expected <= 0.000001 &&
                        (?result < ?expected - 0.000001 ||
                         ?result > ?expected + 0.000001))
                    ||
                    (?expected > 0.000001 &&
                        (?result < ?expected * 0.999999 ||
                         ?result > ?expected * 1.000001))
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:I2_011_BulbousBowAbsentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarClass ;
        sh:class nltl:polarClassValue ;

            nltl:polarClassPc4
            nltl:polarClassPc5
            nltl:polarClassPc6
            nltl:polarClassPc7
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bulbousBowPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:hasValue false ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gen:I2_011_NonPolarClassShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;

    ] ;
```
